    }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool Fire) CheckPressureAndFire(\n        EntityUid uid,\n        FirelockComponent firelock,\n        AirtightComponent airtight)\n        {\n            if (!airtight.AirBlocked)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            if (TryComp(uid, out DockingComponent? dock) && dock.Docked)\n            {\n                // Currently docking automatically opens the doors. But maybe in future, check the pressure difference before opening doors?\n                return (false, false);\n            }\n\n            var xform = Transform(uid);\n            if (!HasComp<GridAtmosphereComponent>(xform.ParentUid))\n                return (false, false);\n\n            var grid = Comp<MapGridComponent>(xform.ParentUid);\n            var pos = _mapping.CoordinatesToTile(xform.ParentUid, grid, xform.Coordinates);\n            var minPressure = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxPressure = float.MinValue;\n            var minTemperature = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxTemperature = float.MinValue;\n            var holdingFire = false;\n            var holdingPressure = false;\n\n            // We cannot simply use `_atmosSystem.GetAdjacentTileMixtures` because of how the `includeBlocked` option\n            // works, we want to ignore the firelock's blocking, while including blockers on other tiles.\n            // GetAdjacentTileMixtures also ignores empty/non-existent tiles, which we don't want. Additionally, for\n            // edge-fire locks, we only want to enumerate over a single directions. So AFAIK there is no nice way of\n            // achieving all this using existing atmos functions, and the functionality is too specialized to bother\n            // adding new public atmos system functions.\n\n            List<Vector2i> tiles = new(4);\n            List<AtmosDirection> directions = new(4);\n            for (var i = 0; i < Atmospherics.Directions; i++)\n            {\n                var dir = (AtmosDirection)(1 << i);\n                if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection.HasFlag(dir))\n                {\n                    directions.Add(dir);\n                    tiles.Add(pos.Offset(dir));\n                }\n            }\n\n            // May also have to consider pressure on the same tile as the firelock.\n            var count = tiles.Count;\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection != AtmosDirection.All)\n                tiles.Add(pos);\n\n            var gasses = _atmosSystem.GetTileMixtures(xform.ParentUid, xform.MapUid, tiles);\n            if (gasses == null)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)\n            {\n                var gas = gasses[i];\n                var dir = directions[i];\n                var adjacentPos = tiles[i];\n\n                if (gas != null)\n                {\n                    // Is there some airtight entity blocking this direction? If yes, don't include this direction in the\n                    // pressure differential\n                    if (HasAirtightBlocker(_mapping.GetAnchoredEntities(xform.ParentUid, grid, adjacentPos), dir.GetOpposite()))\n                        continue;\n\n                    var p = gas.Pressure;\n                    minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                    maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                    minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                    maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                }\n\n                holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n                holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n                if (holdingPressure && holdingFire)\n                    return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n            }\n\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection == AtmosDirection.All)\n                return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n\n            var local = gasses[count];\n            if (local != null)\n            {\n                var p = local.Pressure;\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, local.Temperature);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, local.Temperature);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, 0);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, 0);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, 0);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, 0);\n            }\n\n            holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n            holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n            return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n        }\n\n        private bool HasAirtightBlocker(IEnumerable<EntityUid> enumerable, AtmosDirection dir)\n        {\n            foreach (var ent in enumerable)\n            {\n                if (!_airtightQuery.TryGetComponent(ent, out var airtight) || !airtight.AirBlocked)\n                    continue;\n\n                if ((airtight.AirBlockedDirection & dir) == dir)\n private void OnAtmosAlarm(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent component, AtmosAlarmEvent args)\n        {\n            if (!this.IsPowered(uid, EntityManager))\n                return;\n\n            if (!TryComp<DoorComponent>(uid, out var doorComponent))\n                return;\n\n            if (args.AlarmType == AtmosAlarmType.Normal)\n            {\n                if (doorComponent.State == DoorState.Closed)\n                    _doorSystem.TryOpen(uid);\n            }\n            else if (args.AlarmType == AtmosAlarmType.Danger)\n            {\n                EmergencyPressureStop(uid, component, doorComponent);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool Fire) CheckPressureAndFire(EntityUid uid, FirelockComponent firelock)\n        {\n            if (_airtightQuery.TryGetComponent(uid, out AirtightComponent? airtight))\n                return CheckPressureAndFire(uid, firelock, airtight);\n            return (false, false);\n        }\n\n        public (bool Pressure, bool Fire) CheckPressureAndFire(\n        EntityUid uid,\n        FirelockComponent firelock,\n        AirtightComponent airtight)\n        {\n            if (!airtight.AirBlocked)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            if (TryComp(uid, out DockingComponent? dock) && dock.Docked)\n            {\n                // Currently docking automatically opens the doors. But maybe in future, check the pressure difference before opening doors?\n                return (false, false);\n            }\n\n            var xform = Transform(uid);\n            if (!HasComp<GridAtmosphereComponent>(xform.ParentUid))\n                return (false, false);\n\n            var grid = Comp<MapGridComponent>(xform.ParentUid);\n            var pos = _mapping.CoordinatesToTile(xform.ParentUid, grid, xform.Coordinates);\n            var minPressure = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxPressure = float.MinValue;\n            var minTemperature = float.MaxValue;\n            var maxTemperature = float.MinValue;\n            var holdingFire = false;\n            var holdingPressure = false;\n\n            // We cannot simply use `_atmosSystem.GetAdjacentTileMixtures` because of how the `includeBlocked` option\n            // works, we want to ignore the firelock's blocking, while including blockers on other tiles.\n            // GetAdjacentTileMixtures also ignores empty/non-existent tiles, which we don't want. Additionally, for\n            // edge-fire locks, we only want to enumerate over a single directions. So AFAIK there is no nice way of\n            // achieving all this using existing atmos functions, and the functionality is too specialized to bother\n            // adding new public atmos system functions.\n\n            List<Vector2i> tiles = new(4);\n            List<AtmosDirection> directions = new(4);\n            for (var i = 0; i < Atmospherics.Directions; i++)\n            {\n                var dir = (AtmosDirection)(1 << i);\n                if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection.HasFlag(dir))\n                {\n                    directions.Add(dir);\n                    tiles.Add(pos.Offset(dir));\n                }\n            }\n\n            // May also have to consider pressure on the same tile as the firelock.\n            var count = tiles.Count;\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection != AtmosDirection.All)\n                tiles.Add(pos);\n\n            var gasses = _atmosSystem.GetTileMixtures(xform.ParentUid, xform.MapUid, tiles);\n            if (gasses == null)\n                return (false, false);\n\n            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)\n            {\n                var gas = gasses[i];\n                var dir = directions[i];\n                var adjacentPos = tiles[i];\n\n                if (gas != null)\n                {\n                    // Is there some airtight entity blocking this direction? If yes, don't include this direction in the\n                    // pressure differential\n                    if (HasAirtightBlocker(_mapping.GetAnchoredEntities(xform.ParentUid, grid, adjacentPos), dir.GetOpposite()))\n                        continue;\n\n                    var p = gas.Pressure;\n                    minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                    maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                    minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                    maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, gas.Temperature);\n                }\n\n                holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n                holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n                if (holdingPressure && holdingFire)\n                    return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n            }\n\n            if (airtight.AirBlockedDirection == AtmosDirection.All)\n                return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n\n            var local = gasses[count];\n            if (local != null)\n            {\n                var p = local.Pressure;\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, p);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, p);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, local.Temperature);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, local.Temperature);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                minPressure = Math.Min(minPressure, 0);\n                maxPressure = Math.Max(maxPressure, 0);\n                minTemperature = Math.Min(minTemperature, 0);\n                maxTemperature = Math.Max(maxTemperature, 0);\n            }\n\n            holdingPressure |= maxPressure - minPressure > firelock.PressureThreshold;\n            holdingFire |= maxTemperature - minTemperature > firelock.TemperatureThreshold;\n\n            return (holdingPressure, holdingFire);\n        }\n\n        private bool HasAirtightBlocker(IEnumerable<EntityUid> enumerable, AtmosDirection dir)\n        {\n            foreach (var ent in enumerable)\n            {\n                if (!_airtightQuery.TryGetComponent(ent, out var airtight) || !airtight.AirBlocked)\n                    continue;\n\n                if ((airtight.AirBlockedDirection & dir) == dir)\n                    return true;\n            }\n\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_vrtx_01RN15evjRrx8ikNGv35YBDL","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

door = null, EntityUid? user = null, bool partial = false)\n    {\n        if (!Resolve(uid, ref door))\n            return false;\n\n        // since both closing/closed and welded are door states, we need to prevent 'closing'\n        // a welded door or else there will be weird state bugs\n        if (door.State is DoorState.Welded or DoorState.Closed)\n            return false;\n\n        if (Paused(uid))\n            return false;\n\n        var ev = new BeforeDoorClosedEvent(door.PerformCollisionCheck, partial);\n        RaiseLocalEvent(uid, ev);\n        if (ev.Cancelled)\n            return false;\n\n        if (!HasAccess(uid, user, door))\n            return false;\n\n        return !ev.PerformCollisionCheck || !GetColliding(uid, null, null, door.CheckFixtureCollision, door.PerformCollisionCheck).Any();\n    }\n\n    public void StartClosing(EntityUid uid, DoorComponent? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_vrtx_011ChDh5P6uTQrbMhWyfNz9u","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I fixed six firelock bugs. One caveat first: `recording.mp4` isn't anywhere on disk (`.harbor_assets/` is empty), so I couldn't watch the clip. I found these by reading the firelock code against the expected behaviour. Server and client both build with no errors, and the 40 door/airtight integration tests pass. I haven't run the game to see the changes on screen.\n\n**What ends up on each side of a shut door**\n1. **Air leaked through every shut door** (`Content.Server/Atmos/EntitySystems/AirtightSystem.cs`). When a door went from open to blocking, the game only refreshed the air on that tile when a door opened, not when it shut. So a shut door went on letting gas and pressure through. It now refreshes in both directions.\n2. **The edge firelock sealed the wrong side** (`firelock.yml`). It blocked air on its North edge, but its physical body and its sprite are on the South edge. It now blocks South. This one is visible from the first frame.\n\n**Warning lights**\n3. **The glass firelock couldn't show what kind of danger it was holding** (`firelock.yml`). Its sprite was missing the pressure (cyan) and temperature (orange) warning layers, so it could never show which hazard it was holding back. I added them, matching the standard firelock. Also there from the first frame.\n4. **Warnings stayed on after the door opened** (`SharedFirelockSystem.cs`, `Content.Server/Doors/Systems/FirelockSystem.cs`). The pressure/temperature lights and the red glow were never switched off when a door opened. The firelock now re-checks its warnings every time the door opens or shuts, and switches all of them off unless it's shut.\n\n**How a door moves**\n5. **The door body snapped shut while its lights were still animating** (`Airlocks/Standard/firelock-base.rsi/meta.json`). The standard firelock's closing frames played 10× faster than its opening frames. They now use the same timings as opening.\n6. **Interrupting an opening door replayed the wrong animation** (`Content.Client/Doors/DoorSystem.cs`). If a door was told to shut while still opening, it played the opening animation again. It now plays the closing one.\n\nI also made one small correction in `SharedDoorSystem.cs`: the second half of a door opening was timed with the closing duration instead of the opening one. Every door currently sets both to the same value, so nothing changes on screen today.\n\nIf you can put the video file in place, I'll check these against the clip in case it shows something I couldn't find from the code."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4145,"cache_read_input_tokens":73956,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4145,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f3896161-38b4-4dd7-a415-a0cc0fabd0ec","uuid":"dfca28eb-482e-42bc-82de-baf72888ea8a","timestamp":"2026-11-20T06:17:10.192Z"}
